Privacy Policy
Skyward USA, Inc. ("we," "our," or "us") is committed to protecting the privacy and personal information of our clients and website visitors. This Privacy Policy outlines how we collect, use, and protect your personal information, as well as your rights regarding your data. By using our website, you agree to the practices described in this policy.

1. Information We Collect
We may collect the following types of information from you when you visit our website, contact us, or use our services:

Personal Information: Your name, email address, phone number, and any other information you voluntarily provide when contacting us or filling out forms.
Usage Data: Information about how you interact with our website, such as your IP address, browser type, pages visited, and time spent on each page.
Cookies: Small data files stored on your device to enhance user experience and provide analytics on website usage.
2. How We Use Your Information
We use the information we collect to:

Provide and improve our services.
Respond to your inquiries and provide customer support.
Send updates, newsletters, and promotional materials (only if you have opted in).
Analyze website usage and improve user experience.
Comply with legal obligations.
3. How We Protect Your Information
Your privacy is of utmost importance to us. We implement a variety of security measures to protect your personal information, including:

Encryption of sensitive data.
Secure access controls to prevent unauthorized access.
Regular security audits to ensure the ongoing protection of our systems.
4. Sharing Your Information
We do not sell, trade, or otherwise transfer your personal information to third parties except in the following cases:

If required by law or to comply with a legal obligation.
To trusted service providers who assist us in operating our website and conducting our business, so long as those parties agree to keep the information confidential.
To protect our rights, property, or the safety of Skyward USA, Inc., our users, or others.
5. Your Data Rights
You have the right to access, update, or delete your personal information at any time. To exercise these rights, please contact us at info@skymanhua.org. You also have the right to withdraw your consent to receive promotional communications from us at any time by following the "unsubscribe" instructions provided in our emails.

6. Use of Cookies
Our website uses cookies to enhance user experience, gather analytics, and deliver personalized content. You can choose to disable cookies through your browser settings, but doing so may limit some functionalities of our website.

7. Third-Party Links
Our website may contain links to third-party websites. We are not responsible for the privacy practices or content of these external sites. We encourage you to review their privacy policies before sharing any personal information.

8. Changes to This Privacy Policy
We reserve the right to update or modify this Privacy Policy at any time. Any changes will be posted on this page, and we encourage you to review the policy periodically.

9. Contact Us
If you have any questions or concerns about this Privacy Policy or how your information is handled, please contact us at:

6400 S. Fiddlers Green Circle, Greenwood Village, #250 #1149, CO 80111

Tel: 833-255-0908

Email: info@skymanhua.org

## 🤖 ChatGPT

## Short Answer: **No — not compliant**

Only providing a Google opt-out is **insufficient** for Meta Ads, email, SMS, and general data collection.

You need **multi-layer opt-out mechanisms** across **all data channels**.

---

## What’s REQUIRED (Platform + Law)

### 1. **Email (CAN-SPAM — REQUIRED)**
You must include:
- Unsubscribe link in every email  
- Clear opt-out mechanism  

Source:  
https://www.ftc.gov/business-guidance/resources/can-spam-act-compliance-guide-business  

---

### 2. **SMS (TCPA — CRITICAL)**
You must:
- Obtain **prior express written consent**  
- Provide opt-out (e.g., “Reply STOP to unsubscribe”)  

Source:  
https://www.fcc.gov/general/telemarketing-and-robocalls  

---

### 3. **Meta Ads (REQUIRED)**
Meta requires:
- Disclosure of data collection  
- Explanation of how users can opt out  

Source:  
https://www.facebook.com/business/help/331612538028890  

✔ You should include:
- Link to **Ad Preferences**  
https://www.facebook.com/adpreferences  

---

### 4. **Google (ALREADY INCLUDED — GOOD)**
- https://adssettings.google.com  

---

### 5. **General Cookie Tracking (BEST PRACTICE / REQUIRED IN MANY CASES)**
You should include:
- Cookie banner (accept/reject)  
- Statement: users can disable via browser  

---

## Fix — Add This Section (Plug-and-Play)

### **UPDATED OPT-OUT SECTION (REPLACE YOUR CURRENT ONE)**

---

## 5. YOUR PRIVACY CHOICES & OPT-OUT RIGHTS

You may opt out of data collection and marketing communications through the following methods:

### Email Communications  
You can unsubscribe at any time by clicking the “unsubscribe” link in any email or by contacting us at **support@ascendprimewealth.com**.

### SMS Communications  
You may opt out of SMS messages at any time by replying **STOP** to any message. Message and data rates may apply.

### Meta (Facebook/Instagram Ads)  
You can manage ad preferences or opt out of personalized ads here:  
https://www.facebook.com/adpreferences  

### Google Ads  
You can control how Google uses your data for ads here:  
https://adssettings.google.com  

### Cookies & Tracking  
You can disable cookies through your browser settings. Note that some features of our website may not function properly if cookies are disabled.

---

**After fix:** ✅ Compliant with:

## 👤 You

do i have to put the date of the policy?

---

## 🤖 ChatGPT

## Short Answer: **Yes — required (practically + for audits)**

Not always explicitly mandated by statute, but **required in practice** for:

---

## Why It’s Necessary

### 1. **FTC Compliance (Deceptive Practices Risk)**
Under entity["law","Federal Trade Commission Act","FTC Act Section 5"]:
- Policies must be **clear and not misleading**
- Without a date → users cannot know if terms are current  
→ This can be viewed as **deceptive or inadequate disclosure**

---

### 2. **Meta & Google Ads Approval**
Both require:
- **“Up-to-date and accurate” privacy policy**

If no date:
- Fails manual review more often  
- Weakens compliance signal  

---

### 3. **Legal Defensibility (Critical)**
If dispute / complaint / lawsuit:
- You must prove **which version of policy was active**
- No date = **no evidentiary anchor**

---

### 4. **CCPA / State Laws (Best Practice Standard)**
While not explicitly requiring a date line:
- Regulators expect **version clarity + update transparency**

---

## What to Use (Correct Format)

### ✅ Best Practice:
**Effective Date: March 17, 2026**

Optional upgrade:
- “Last Updated: March 17, 2026”

---

## Final Verdict

| Scenario | Status |
|--------|-------|
| No date | ❌ Weak / risky |
| Effective date only | ✅ Compliant |
| Effective + Last Updated | ✅ Strongest |

---

## Bottom Line
Include the date.  
It’s a **low-effort, high-leverage legal protection** and improves:
- Ad approvals  
- Audit readiness  
- Legal defensibility  

---

If you want next level: I can version-control your policy + create update protocol so you’re protected long-term.

## 👤 You

is there any problems with no  commas and only space breaks?

---

## 🤖 ChatGPT

## Short Answer: **Yes — that creates real compliance risk**

Not illegal by itself, but **can fail audits, ads review, and legal clarity standards**.

---

## Why It’s a Problem

### 1. **FTC Clarity Requirement**
Under entity["law","Federal Trade Commission Act","FTC Act Section 5"]:
- Disclosures must be **“clear and conspicuous”**

If you remove commas:
- Sentences become ambiguous  
- Meaning can change  
→ Risk: **considered unclear or misleading**

---

### 2. **Meta + Google Policy Review**
Both platforms require:
- **Readable, structured, unambiguous policies**

No commas =  
- Looks auto-generated / low quality  
- Higher chance of:
  - Ad rejection  
  - Manual review flags  

---

### 3. **Legal Interpretation Risk (Biggest Issue)**
Contracts and policies rely on:
- **Precise punctuation**

Without commas:
- Clauses can be misinterpreted  
- Liability exposure increases  

Example:
- “We collect email phone number and data”  
→ unclear grouping  
→ weakens enforceability  

---

### 4. **Audit / Dispute Scenario**
If challenged:
- Opposing side can argue:
  - “Policy is vague / unclear / unenforceable”  

That alone can weaken:
- TCPA consent defense  
- Data usage disclosures  
- Ad compliance defense  

---

## What IS Safe

### ✅ Correct Structure:
- Proper sentences with punctuation  
- Line breaks for readability  
- Bullet points + sections  

### ❌ Risky:
- No commas  
- Run-on formatting  
- “Minimalist” legal writing  

---

## Final Verdict

| Format | Status |
|------|-------|
| No commas | ❌ Risky |
| Proper punctuation | ✅ Required |
| Clean + structured | ✅ Best |

---

## Bottom Line
Keep commas.  
This is **not stylistic — it directly affects legal enforceability and ad compliance**.

---

**without sacrificing compliance**

## 👤 You

how can we list terms and conditions and terms of service simultaneously so no confusion

---

## 🤖 ChatGPT

## Short Answer: **Use ONE document, define both names**

Do **not create two separate documents**.  
That creates ambiguity and weakens enforceability.

---

# ✅ Correct Structure (Industry Standard)

### Title it:
```text
Terms of Service and Terms and Conditions
```

### Then define it ONCE:

```text
These Terms of Service and Terms and Conditions (“Terms”) govern your use of our services.
```

✔ From that point forward → only use **“Terms”**

---

# ❌ What NOT to do

- Separate “Terms of Service” page  
- Separate “Terms and Conditions” page  
- Switching between names randomly  

→ This creates:
- Legal ambiguity  
- Weakens enforcement  
- Confuses users and courts  

---

# 🧠 Why This Matters

Courts and disputes look for:
- **One governing agreement**
- Clear definition of terms

If you split:
- Opponent can argue:
  → “Which terms apply?”  
  → “They are inconsistent”  

---

# 🔒 Best Practice (Bulletproof)

## Header:
```text
ASCEND PRIME WEALTH LLC — TERMS OF SERVICE AND TERMS AND CONDITIONS
```

## Opening clause:
```text
These Terms of Service and Terms and Conditions (“Terms”) govern your access to and use of our services.
```

✔ Done — now legally unified

---

# 🎯 Final Verdict

| Approach | Status |
|----------|--------|
| One combined doc (defined as “Terms”) | ✅ Correct |
| Two separate docs | ❌ Risk |
| Mixed naming without definition | ❌ Weak |

---

## Bottom Line
- Use **both names once**
- Define them
- Then simplify to **“Terms”**

That’s the **cleanest, strongest, and most defensible structure**.

---

If you want next level:
I can add **checkout checkbox language + enforceable acceptance clause** (this is what actually wins disputes).

again this is public facing, which will be different from contract at time of signing that has more specifications. This is more so for public facing compliance and professionalism

> COACHING SERVICES AGREEMENT
> This Coaching Services Agreement (“Agreement”) is entered into as of __________, by and between:
> Coach: Kai Bax, with principal business address at 11 Barrack Square, 6000 (“Coach”),
> And…
> Client: _______________ , residing at _______________________ (“Client”).
> 1. Scope of Services
> Coach agrees to provide coaching services to Client for the purpose of assisting Client in achieving personal,
> professional, and business objectives, as mutually agreed upon (“Services”). Services shall include:
> ● One (1) 60-minute 1-on-1 onboarding session via Zoom upon joining.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with our Sales Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Complete access to training modules on the Skool platform for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Client shall have daily access to the Coach via the group community on the Skool platform for a period of
> four (4) months. The Coach will use commercially reasonable efforts to respond to all questions within
> twenty-four (24) hours.
> 2. Fees and Payment
> ● Fee: Client shall pay Coach a total amount of USD 6,800 for the Services.
> ● Payment Due Date: Payment shall be made in full before commencement of Services, unless otherwise
> agreed in writing.
> ● No Refunds: All fees are non-refundable for any reason, including but not limited to early termination by
> Client, non-attendance, or dissatisfaction with results (subject to Clause 5).
> 3. Cancellations and Rescheduling
> Client must provide at least 12 hours’ prior notice to reschedule a session. Sessions missed without such notice shall
> be deemed forfeited and will not be rescheduled.
> 4. No Guarantee of Results
> Coach does not warrant or guarantee any specific outcomes from the Services. Client acknowledges that results are
> dependent upon Client’s personal effort, actions, and implementation.
> 5. 7-Day Love It or Leave It Guarantee
> Client may cancel this Agreement for any reason within seven (7) calendar days from the Effective Date (“Guarantee
> Period”) by providing written notice to Coach. If such notice is received within the Guarantee Period, Coach shall
> issue a full refund of all amounts paid under this Agreement within seven (7) days of receiving notice. After the
> expiration of the Guarantee Period, all fees paid shall be non-refundable for any reason.
> 02 / 03 / 2026
> Alan Nguyen, Prismatic 10700 France Ave S Ste 110, Bloomingt
> Doc ID: 36474df7a29e92bf63869e8b60f75d604bed108d
> 
> <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 2 / 3>
> 
> 6. Confidentiality
> Coach shall maintain the confidentiality of all information disclosed by Client during the course of Services, except as
> required by law or with Client’s prior written consent.
> 7. Termination
> Client may terminate this Agreement at any time by providing written notice to the Coach. Upon termination, no
> refunds shall be issued for amounts paid (subject to Clause 5).
> 8. Governing Law
> This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Perth, Western
> Australia, without regard to its conflict of law provisions.
> IN WITNESS WHEREOF, the parties hereto have executed this Agreement as of the date first written above.
> Coach: ___________________________ Date: ___________
> Client: ___________________________ Date: ___________

> 16.2 TERMINATION BY THE CLIENT
> The Client may terminate this Agreement in whole or in part by written notice to ScaleClients.io if
> ScaleClients.io:
> (a) has committed a material breach of this Agreement and has failed to remedy the breach
> within 30 days after receiving written notice from the Client; or
> (b) consents to such termination, subject to the Client’s fulfilment of any pre-conditions to
> such consent (for example, payment of a pro-rata portion of the agreed fees).
> 16.3 EFFECT OF TERMINATION
> Upon termination of this Agreement, the Client must promptly pay (at the ScaleClients.io’s request):
> (a) the Cancellation Fee specified in the Client Form;
> (b) any payments required by ScaleClients.io to third party suppliers or ScaleClients.io to
> discontinue their work;
> (c) ScaleClients.io’s standard fees in relation to work already performed; and/or
> (d) an equitable amount by way of profit margin on the preceding items.
> 16.4 SURVIVAL
> Any clause that by its nature would reasonably be expected to be performed after the termination
> or expiry of this Agreement will survive and be enforceable after such termination or expiry.
> 17 DISPUTE RESOLUTION
> (a) A party claiming that a dispute has arisen under or in connection with this agreement
> must not commence court proceedings arising from or relating to the dispute, other than a
> claim for urgent interlocutory relief, unless that party has complied with the requirements
> of this clause.
> (b) A party that requires resolution of a dispute which arises under or in connection with this
> agreement must give the other party or parties to the dispute written notice containing
> reasonable details of the dispute and requiring its resolution under this clause.
> (c) Once the dispute notice has been given, each party to the dispute must then use its best
> efforts to resolve the dispute in good faith. If the dispute is not resolved within a period of
> 14 days (or such other period as agreed by the parties in writing) after the date of the
> notice, any party to the dispute may take legal proceedings to resolve the dispute.
> 918 NOTICES
> A notice or other communication to a party under this agreement must be:
> (a) in writing and in English; and
> (b) delivered via email to the other party, to the email address specified in this agreement, or
> if no email address is specified in this agreement, then the email address most regularly
> used by the parties to correspond for the purposes of the subject matter of this agreement
> as at the date of this agreement (Email Address). The parties may update their Email
> Address by notice to the other party.
> (c) Unless the party sending the notice knows or reasonably ought to suspect that an email
> was not delivered to the other party’s Email Address, notice will be taken to be given:
> (i) 24 hours after the email was sent, unless that falls on a Saturday, Sunday or a
> public holiday in the state or territory whose laws govern this Agreement, in
> which case the notice will be taken to be given on the next occurring Business
> Day in that state or territory; or
> (ii) when replied to by the other party,
> whichever is earlier.
> 19 GENERAL
> 19.1 GOVERNING LAW AND JURISDICTION
> This agreement is governed by the law applying in Western Australia, Australia. Each party
> irrevocably submits to the exclusive jurisdiction of the courts of Western Australia, Australia and
> courts of appeal from them in respect of any proceedings arising out of or in connection with this
> agreement. Each party irrevocably waives any objection to the venue of any legal process on the
> basis that the process has been brought in an inconvenient forum.

> 1 CLIENT FORM, THIS AGREEMENT
> (a) These Client Terms will apply to all the Client’s dealings with ScaleClients.io, including
> being incorporated in all agreements, quotations or orders under which ScaleClients.io is
> to provide services to the Client (each a ‘Client Form’) together with any additional terms
> included in such Client Form (provided such additional terms are recorded in writing).
> (b) The Client will be taken to have accepted this Agreement if the Client accepts a Client
> Form, or if the Client orders, accepts or pays for any services provided by ScaleClients.io
> after receiving or becoming aware of this Agreement or these Client Terms.
> (c) In the event of any inconsistency between these Client Terms and any Client Form, the
> clauses of these Client Terms will prevail to the extent of such inconsistency, except that
> any “Special Conditions” (being terms described as such in a Client Form) will prevail
> over these Client Terms to the extent of any inconsistency.
> 2 SERVICES
> 2.1 GENERALLY
> (a) In consideration for the payment of the fees set out in the Client Form (Fees),
> ScaleClients.io will provide the Client with the services set out in a Client Form
> (Services).
> (b) ScaleClients.io will provide the Services during the Work Times set out in a Client Form,
> and for any additional hours agreed in writing by the parties.
> (c) Unless otherwise agreed in writing, the ScaleClients.io may, in its discretion:
> (i) not commence work on any Services until the Client has paid any Fees or
> deposit payable in respect of such Services; and
> (ii) withhold delivery of Services until the Client has paid an invoice in respect of
> any Services, including invoices for previous Services that have already been
> provided.
> 2.2 SERVICES
> The Client agrees that:
> (a) the Client must make themselves available for a 1-on-1 call at the beginning of the
> Project Period, in which ScaleClients.io will explain the Client Acquisition Coaching

> This Coaching Services Agreement (“Agreement”) is entered into as of __________, by and between:
> Coach: Kai Bax, with principal business address at 11 Barrack Square, 6000 (“Coach”),

> Client: _______________ , residing at _______________________ (“Client”).
> 1. Scope of Services
> Coach agrees to provide coaching services to Client for the purpose of assisting Client in achieving personal,
> professional, and business objectives, as mutually agreed upon (“Services”). Services shall include:
> ● One (1) 60-minute 1-on-1 onboarding session via Zoom upon joining.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with our Sales Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Complete access to training modules on the Skool platform for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Client shall have daily access to the Coach via the group community on the Skool platform for a period of
> four (4) months. The Coach will use commercially reasonable efforts to respond to all questions within
> twenty-four (24) hours.
> 2. Fees and Payment
> ● Fee: Client shall pay Coach a total amount of USD 6,800 for the Services.
> ● Payment Due Date: Payment shall be made in full before commencement of Services, unless otherwise
> agreed in writing.
> ● No Refunds: All fees are non-refundable for any reason, including but not limited to early termination by
> Client, non-attendance, or dissatisfaction with results (subject to Clause 5).
> 3. Cancellations and Rescheduling
> Client must provide at least 12 hours’ prior notice to reschedule a session. Sessions missed without such notice shall
> be deemed forfeited and will not be rescheduled.
> 4. No Guarantee of Results
> Coach does not warrant or guarantee any specific outcomes from the Services. Client acknowledges that results are
> dependent upon Client’s personal effort, actions, and implementation.
> 5. 7-Day Love It or Leave It Guarantee
> Client may cancel this Agreement for any reason within seven (7) calendar days from the Effective Date (“Guarantee
> Period”) by providing written notice to Coach. If such notice is received within the Guarantee Period, Coach shall
> issue a full refund of all amounts paid under this Agreement within seven (7) days of receiving notice. After the
> expiration of the Guarantee Period, all fees paid shall be non-refundable for any reason.

> Alan Nguyen, Prismatic 10700 France Ave S Ste 110, Bloomingt

> 8. Governing Law
> This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Perth, Western

## 🤖 ChatGPT

Yes. I reviewed the agreements, and the right move is **not** to make your public-facing terms look like a full enforcement contract. Public pages should be **clean, credible, and clear**, while the harder-edged protections live in the checkout terms and signed agreement. That gives you better FTC posture, better client trust, and still strong protection. The FTC’s core standard is that material disclosures must be **clear and conspicuous**, and commercial email/SMS terms must give users real stop/unsubscribe rights. citeturn158253view0turn158253view1turn158253view3turn740810search0

## What the uploaded agreements do well

The **best structural model** is the ScaleClients approach: a short offer summary plus master terms, express acceptance by signing/order/payment/use, a rule for what controls if special conditions conflict, clear “not legal/financial advice” language, conditional guarantee criteria, payment processor language, confidentiality, IP ownership, limitation of liability, notices, severance, and governing law. That is the strongest base to borrow from for your public docs. fileciteturn2file0 fileciteturn2file1

The **best guarantee style** is the simpler, client-readable version used in the ScaleClients DWY and Freedom Funders materials: short promise, objective conditions, and a defined time window. That reads more credible than a giant wall of legalese and is easier to defend if the conditions are genuinely disclosed before purchase. fileciteturn1file12 fileciteturn0file2

## What I would not copy into the public-facing version

The aged shelf agreement’s “no refunds under any circumstances” and one-day acceptance window are too harsh for a public website and are better kept, if at all, in a very specific signed fulfillment document. fileciteturn1file10

Prime Mover’s public terms are protective, but several provisions are too aggressive for your stated goal of staying compliant without spooking prospects: blanket no-refund language, automatic renewal, company-selected arbitrator, a one-year arbitration deadline, and a class-action waiver. Those can be enforceable in some contexts, but on a public page they increase friction and can feel one-sided. If you ever use recurring billing, the FTC’s current negative-option framework requires clear disclosure of material terms and cancellation that is as easy as signup. fileciteturn2file2 citeturn740810search0turn740810search2

Because you are in a **business coaching / funding / money-making adjacent** space, I would also keep your public docs and public marketing very disciplined around results claims. The FTC has specifically proposed expanding business opportunity rules to cover money-making opportunities like business coaching and investment opportunities, and has emphasized substantiation for earnings-type claims. That proposal is not the same as a final rule for your business today, but it is a strong signal of where scrutiny is headed. citeturn158253view4

## My recommendation: public-facing stack vs signed contract stack

| Layer | Public website | Checkout / signed agreement |
|---|---|---|
| Terms & Conditions | Yes | Short acceptance reference |
| Privacy Policy | Yes | Optional reference |
| Refund Policy | Yes | Yes, offer-specific override |
| SMS Terms / Consent | Yes | Yes |
| Earnings / Results Disclaimer | Yes | Yes |
| Offer-specific scope, deliverables, deadlines, guarantee conditions | Summary only | Full detail |
| Chargeback procedure, collections, legal costs, arbitration mechanics | Light version | Full version |
| Client representations, detailed cooperation duties, document deadlines | Light version | Full version |

That split mirrors what the better agreements are already doing: public clarity first, operational specifics later. fileciteturn2file0 fileciteturn1file12

## Exact changes I would make to your public terms

1. **Use one master public document** titled “Terms & Conditions” or “Terms of Service and Terms & Conditions,” then define it once as “Terms.”  
2. **Do not lead with “all sales final.”** Use: “Refund eligibility, if any, is governed by our written Refund Policy and any offer-specific terms presented at checkout.”  
3. **Keep results disclaimers, but make them human.** Example: “We do not guarantee any specific credit, funding, revenue, or business outcome.” That is cleaner than aggressive anti-reliance wording and better aligned with FTC clarity expectations. citeturn158253view0turn158253view4  
4. **If you offer a guarantee, summarize it publicly and fully define it at checkout.** Public page: plain-English summary. Checkout/order form: exact prerequisites, evidence, timeline, exclusions. That is the best lesson from ScaleClients and Freedom Funders. fileciteturn2file0 fileciteturn0file2  
5. **Keep third-party processor/platform language.** You want Stripe/GoHighLevel/other provider references, plus a sentence that their terms may also apply. ScaleClients does this well. fileciteturn2file1  
6. **Keep confidentiality, IP ownership, and a reasonable license to client content.** Those are strong and normal. fileciteturn2file1  
7. **Keep a liability cap, but make it measured.** Public-facing: cap liability to amounts paid for the specific service at issue, to the extent permitted by law. Avoid screaming all-caps unless necessary. ScaleClients’ version is more balanced than Prime Mover’s. fileciteturn2file1 fileciteturn2file2  
8. **Use a dispute notice / cure process in the public terms.** Example: written notice first, 14-day informal resolution window, then arbitration or court as chosen in the signed agreement. That is more professional and less alarming than immediate hard-arbitration language on the site. fileciteturn2file1  
9. **If you use marketing email or SMS, keep those disclosures out of generic terms alone.** Also maintain a separate Privacy Policy and SMS Terms page. CAN-SPAM requires unsubscribe rights, and FCC rules require consent/revocation handling for covered marketing texts. Consumers may revoke through reasonable means, and requests must be honored promptly, no later than ten business days. citeturn158253view1turn158253view3  
10. **Avoid automatic renewal unless you truly need it.** If you use it, disclose price, frequency, renewal timing, and cancellation mechanism before signup, and make cancellation easy. citeturn740810search0turn740810search2  

## Best tone for your public terms

Use this posture:

- firm
- plain English
- specific
- not threatening
- not absolute unless truly true

Bad public tone:
- “No refunds under any circumstances”
- “You waive everything”
- “We choose the arbitrator”
- “Any claim after one year is barred”

Better public tone:
- “Any refund rights are limited to those expressly stated in our Refund Policy or offer-specific written terms”
- “We do not guarantee outcomes”
- “Before filing a dispute, you agree to notify us and allow a reasonable opportunity to resolve the issue”
- “To the fullest extent permitted by law, liability is limited to amounts paid for the applicable service”

## My recommendation for the next chunk

Next, I draft your **public-facing Terms & Conditions v2** using this structure:

1. Agreement to Terms  
2. Services / Educational Nature / No Professional Advice  
3. Eligibility and Lawful Use  
4. Payments and Billing  
5. Refunds and Cancellations  
6. Results / Earnings / Funding Disclaimer  
7. Client Responsibilities  
8. Third-Party Platforms and Processors  
9. Intellectual Property  
10. Testimonials / Case Studies / No Promise of Similar Results  
11. Communications / Email / SMS  
12. Disclaimer of Warranties  
13. Limitation of Liability  
14. Indemnification  
15. Dispute Resolution  
16. Governing Law  
17. Changes to Terms  
18. Contact

If you want, I’ll do **Chunk 2 now** and draft that full public-facing Terms & Conditions in final language.

> This agreement embodies the entire agreement between the parties and supersedes any prior

> COACHING SERVICES AGREEMENT
> This Coaching Services Agreement (“Agreement”) is entered into as of __________, by and between:
> Coach: Kai Bax, with principal business address at 11 Barrack Square, 6000 (“Coach”),
> And…
> Client: _______________ , residing at _______________________ (“Client”).
> 1. Scope of Services
> Coach agrees to provide coaching services to Client for the purpose of assisting Client in achieving personal,
> professional, and business objectives, as mutually agreed upon (“Services”). Services shall include:
> ● One (1) 60-minute 1-on-1 onboarding session via Zoom upon joining.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with our Sales Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Complete access to training modules on the Skool platform for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Client shall have daily access to the Coach via the group community on the Skool platform for a period of
> four (4) months. The Coach will use commercially reasonable efforts to respond to all questions within
> twenty-four (24) hours.
> 2. Fees and Payment
> ● Fee: Client shall pay Coach a total amount of USD 6,800 for the Services.
> ● Payment Due Date: Payment shall be made in full before commencement of Services, unless otherwise
> agreed in writing.
> ● No Refunds: All fees are non-refundable for any reason, including but not limited to early termination by
> Client, non-attendance, or dissatisfaction with results (subject to Clause 5).
> 3. Cancellations and Rescheduling
> Client must provide at least 12 hours’ prior notice to reschedule a session. Sessions missed without such notice shall
> be deemed forfeited and will not be rescheduled.
> 4. No Guarantee of Results
> Coach does not warrant or guarantee any specific outcomes from the Services. Client acknowledges that results are
> dependent upon Client’s personal effort, actions, and implementation.
> 5. 7-Day Love It or Leave It Guarantee
> Client may cancel this Agreement for any reason within seven (7) calendar days from the Effective Date (“Guarantee
> Period”) by providing written notice to Coach. If such notice is received within the Guarantee Period, Coach shall
> issue a full refund of all amounts paid under this Agreement within seven (7) days of receiving notice. After the
> expiration of the Guarantee Period, all fees paid shall be non-refundable for any reason.
> 02 / 03 / 2026
> Alan Nguyen, Prismatic 10700 France Ave S Ste 110, Bloomingt
> Doc ID: 36474df7a29e92bf63869e8b60f75d604bed108d6. Confidentiality

> 7. Termination
> Client may terminate this Agreement at any time by providing written notice to the Coach. Upon termination, no
> refunds shall be issued for amounts paid (subject to Clause 5).
> 8. Governing Law
> This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Perth, Western
> Australia, without regard to its conflict of law provisions.

> 8(c) (Consequential loss) ScaleClients.io will not be liable for any incidental, special or

> 15 SUBCONTRACTING

> 16 TERMINATION
> 16.1 TERMINATION BY BAX CONSULTING
> ScaleClients.io may terminate this Agreement in whole or in part immediately by written notice to
> the Client if:
> (a) the Client is in breach of any term of this Agreement; or
> (b) the Client becomes subject to any form of insolvency or bankruptcy administration.
> 16.2 TERMINATION BY THE CLIENT
> The Client may terminate this Agreement in whole or in part by written notice to ScaleClients.io if
> ScaleClients.io:
> (a) has committed a material breach of this Agreement and has failed to remedy the breach
> within 30 days after receiving written notice from the Client; or
> (b) consents to such termination, subject to the Client’s fulfilment of any pre-conditions to
> such consent (for example, payment of a pro-rata portion of the agreed fees).
> 16.3 EFFECT OF TERMINATION
> Upon termination of this Agreement, the Client must promptly pay (at the ScaleClients.io’s request):
> (a) the Cancellation Fee specified in the Client Form;
> (b) any payments required by ScaleClients.io to third party suppliers or ScaleClients.io to
> discontinue their work;
> (c) ScaleClients.io’s standard fees in relation to work already performed; and/or
> (d) an equitable amount by way of profit margin on the preceding items.
> 16.4 SURVIVAL
> Any clause that by its nature would reasonably be expected to be performed after the termination
> or expiry of this Agreement will survive and be enforceable after such termination or expiry.
> 17 DISPUTE RESOLUTION
> (a) A party claiming that a dispute has arisen under or in connection with this agreement
> must not commence court proceedings arising from or relating to the dispute, other than a
> claim for urgent interlocutory relief, unless that party has complied with the requirements
> of this clause.
> (b) A party that requires resolution of a dispute which arises under or in connection with this
> agreement must give the other party or parties to the dispute written notice containing
> reasonable details of the dispute and requiring its resolution under this clause.

> efforts to resolve the dispute in good faith.

> professional, and business objectives, as mutually agreed upon (“Services”). Services shall include:
> ● One (1) 60-minute 1-on-1 onboarding session via Zoom upon joining.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with our Sales Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Complete access to training modules on the Skool platform for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Client shall have daily access to the Coach via the group community on the Skool platform for a period of
> four (4) months. The Coach will use commercially reasonable efforts to respond to all questions within
> twenty-four (24) hours.
> 2. Fees and Payment
> ● Fee: Client shall pay Coach a total amount of USD 6,800 for the Services.
> ● Payment Due Date: Payment shall be made in full before commencement of Services, unless otherwise
> agreed in writing.
> ● No Refunds: All fees are non-refundable for any reason, including but not limited to early termination by
> Client, non-attendance, or dissatisfaction with results (subject to Clause 5).
> 3. Cancellations and Rescheduling
> Client must provide at least 12 hours’ prior notice to reschedule a session. Sessions missed without such notice shall
> be deemed forfeited and will not be rescheduled.
> 4. No Guarantee of Results
> Coach does not warrant or guarantee any specific outcomes from the Services. Client acknowledges that results are
> dependent upon Client’s personal effort, actions, and implementation.
> 5. 7-Day Love It or Leave It Guarantee
> Client may cancel this Agreement for any reason within seven (7) calendar days from the Effective Date (“Guarantee
> Period”) by providing written notice to Coach. If such notice is received within the Guarantee Period, Coach shall
> issue a full refund of all amounts paid under this Agreement within seven (7) days of receiving notice. After the
> expiration of the Guarantee Period, all fees paid shall be non-refundable for any reason.
> 02 / 03 / 2026
> Alan Nguyen, Prismatic 10700 France Ave S Ste 110, Bloomingt
> Doc ID: 36474df7a29e92bf63869e8b60f75d604bed108d6. Confidentiality
> Coach shall maintain the confidentiality of all information disclosed by Client during the course of Services, except as
> required by law or with Client’s prior written consent.
> 7. Termination
> Client may terminate this Agreement at any time by providing written notice to the Coach. Upon termination, no
> refunds shall be issued for amounts paid (subject to Clause 5).
> 8. Governing Law
> This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Perth, Western
> Australia, without regard to its conflict of law provisions.
> IN WITNESS WHEREOF, the parties hereto have executed this Agreement as of the date first written above.

> CLIENT TERMS
> These Client Terms, together with any Client Form (defined in clause 1), set out the agreement (this
> ‘Agreement’) under the terms of which Bax Consulting and Marketing Pty Ltd (ABN: 94 644 820 128)
> (‘ScaleClients.io’) provides Services (defined in clause 2) to you or the company which you represent (the
> ‘Client’).
> 1 CLIENT FORM, THIS AGREEMENT
> (a) These Client Terms will apply to all the Client’s dealings with ScaleClients.io, including
> being incorporated in all agreements, quotations or orders under which ScaleClients.io is
> to provide services to the Client (each a ‘Client Form’) together with any additional terms
> included in such Client Form (provided such additional terms are recorded in writing).
> (b) The Client will be taken to have accepted this Agreement if the Client accepts a Client
> Form, or if the Client orders, accepts or pays for any services provided by ScaleClients.io
> after receiving or becoming aware of this Agreement or these Client Terms.
> (c) In the event of any inconsistency between these Client Terms and any Client Form, the
> clauses of these Client Terms will prevail to the extent of such inconsistency, except that
> any “Special Conditions” (being terms described as such in a Client Form) will prevail
> over these Client Terms to the extent of any inconsistency.
> 2 SERVICES
> 2.1 GENERALLY
> (a) In consideration for the payment of the fees set out in the Client Form (Fees),
> ScaleClients.io will provide the Client with the services set out in a Client Form
> (Services).
> (b) ScaleClients.io will provide the Services during the Work Times set out in a Client Form,
> and for any additional hours agreed in writing by the parties.
> (c) Unless otherwise agreed in writing, the ScaleClients.io may, in its discretion:
> (i) not commence work on any Services until the Client has paid any Fees or
> deposit payable in respect of such Services; and
> (ii) withhold delivery of Services until the Client has paid an invoice in respect of
> any Services, including invoices for previous Services that have already been
> provided.
> 2.2 SERVICES
> The Client agrees that:
> (a) the Client must make themselves available for a 1-on-1 call at the beginning of the
> Project Period, in which ScaleClients.io will explain the Client Acquisition Coaching
> Services roadmap, and the materials and services that will be provided as part of the
> Services throughout the Project Period;
> (b) during the Project Period, ScaleClients.io will make themselves available to the Client for
> 1 phone call every 2 weeks in which ScaleClients.io coaches will answer specific
> questions the Client might have relating to the Services;
> (c) the materials that ScaleClients.io provides to the Client as part of the Client Acquisition
> Coaching Services will be made available to the Client in perpetuity, however the Client
> will need to contact ScaleClients.io to request a new copy of these materials if they are
> lost or inaccessible for whatever reason to the Client, and ScaleClients.io will endeavour
> to provide those materials to the Client again within 14 days; and
> (d) the Client must make a reasonable effort to engage and attend the services provided by
> ScaleClients.io as part of the Client Acquisition Coaching Services, including that the
> Client must:
> 3

> (i) attend a minimum of 1 call as described in clauses 2.2(b) and 2.2(c) per week;
> and
> (ii) complete 90% of the video materials provided as part of the Client Acquisition
> Coaching Services by the end of the Project Period.
> 3 DISCLAIMERS – NO LEGAL OR FINANCIAL ADVICE
> (a) All information provided by ScaleClients.io as part of the Services is general information.
> (b) This information is based on information you provide to ScaleClients.io.
> (c) No information provided as part of the Services is intended to be legal or financial advice
> of any kind and it should not be relied on as such.
> (d) You should obtain specific financial, legal or other professional advice before relying on
> the Services. By not seeking such advice, you accept the risk that the information
> provided as part of the Services may not meet the specific needs of your business.
> 4 CONDITIONAL GUARANTEE
> (a) Subject to clause 4(b), ScaleClients.io guarantees that ScaleClients.io will refund the
> Fees to the Client if, within the Project Period, the Client does not gain 50 qualified
> booked appointments (Guarantee).
> (b) In order for the Client to be eligible for the Guarantee:
> (i) the Client must provide clear evidence that the 50 qualified booked
> appointments were either 1. not achieved or 2. not qualified. This clear evidence
> needs to be clear proof from the scheduling software the Client uses which
> proves the 50 qualified appointments were not achieved; and
> (ii) the Client must:
> (A) script and record a VSL based on the exact framework ScaleClients.io
> provides, within 30 days of signing this contract;
> (B) record the video ad scripts provided to the Client by ScaleClients.io,
> within 30 days of signing this contract; and
> (C) spend (a minimum) $166 (USD) per day consecutively for 90 days,
> done for the Client by ScaleClients.io.
> (c) If the above stipulations are met, and the Client does not recieve 50 qualified
> appointments after 90 days of ScaleClients.io running the ads at $166 USD per day
> consecutively, the Client will receive a full refund for the total Fee amount ($18,000 USD)
> from ScaleClients.io. The Client will receive this refund on day 120 (after signing this
> agreement) if 50 qualified appointments were not achieved after implementing the above
> stipulations. The client will receive the refund after Stripe has processed the payment
> (usually takes 10 business days from Stripe).
> (d) Initial to acknowledge conditions with regard to the guarantee:
> 5 CLIENT OBLIGATIONS
> 5.1 PROVIDE INFORMATION AND LIAISON
> (a) The Client must provide ScaleClients.io with all documentation, information and
> assistance reasonably required for ScaleClients.io to perform the Services.
> (b) The Client must provide to ScaleClients.io all information reasonably required by

> COACHING SERVICES AGREEMENT
> This Coaching Services Agreement (“Agreement”) is entered into as of __________, by and between:
> Coach: Kai Bax, with principal business address at 11 Barrack Square, 6000 (“Coach”),
> And…
> Client: _______________ , residing at _______________________ (“Client”).
> 1. Scope of Services
> Coach agrees to provide coaching services to Client for the purpose of assisting Client in achieving personal,
> professional, and business objectives, as mutually agreed upon (“Services”). Services shall include:
> ● One (1) 60-minute 1-on-1 onboarding session via Zoom upon joining.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with our Sales Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Complete access to training modules on the Skool platform for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Client shall have daily access to the Coach via the group community on the Skool platform for a period of
> four (4) months. The Coach will use commercially reasonable efforts to respond to all questions within
> twenty-four (24) hours.
> 2. Fees and Payment
> ● Fee: Client shall pay Coach a total amount of USD 6,800 for the Services.
> ● Payment Due Date: Payment shall be made in full before commencement of Services, unless otherwise
> agreed in writing.
> ● No Refunds: All fees are non-refundable for any reason, including but not limited to early termination by
> Client, non-attendance, or dissatisfaction with results (subject to Clause 5).
> 3. Cancellations and Rescheduling
> Client must provide at least 12 hours’ prior notice to reschedule a session. Sessions missed without such notice shall
> be deemed forfeited and will not be rescheduled.
> 4. No Guarantee of Results
> Coach does not warrant or guarantee any specific outcomes from the Services. Client acknowledges that results are
> dependent upon Client’s personal effort, actions, and implementation.
> 5. 7-Day Love It or Leave It Guarantee
> Client may cancel this Agreement for any reason within seven (7) calendar days from the Effective Date (“Guarantee
> Period”) by providing written notice to Coach. If such notice is received within the Guarantee Period, Coach shall
> issue a full refund of all amounts paid under this Agreement within seven (7) days of receiving notice. After the
> expiration of the Guarantee Period, all fees paid shall be non-refundable for any reason.
> 02 / 03 / 2026
> Alan Nguyen, Prismatic 10700 France Ave S Ste 110, Bloomingt
> Doc ID: 36474df7a29e92bf63869e8b60f75d604bed108d
> 
> <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 2 / 3>
> 
> 6. Confidentiality
> Coach shall maintain the confidentiality of all information disclosed by Client during the course of Services, except as
> required by law or with Client’s prior written consent.
> 7. Termination
> Client may terminate this Agreement at any time by providing written notice to the Coach. Upon termination, no
> refunds shall be issued for amounts paid (subject to Clause 5).
> 8. Governing Law
> This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Perth, Western
> Australia, without regard to its conflict of law provisions.
> IN WITNESS WHEREOF, the parties hereto have executed this Agreement as of the date first written above.

# ASCEND PRIME WEALTH LLC  
## TERMS OF SERVICE AND TERMS & CONDITIONS

**Effective Date:** March 17, 2026

These Terms of Service and Terms & Conditions (“Terms”) govern your access to and use of the websites, funnels, forms, communications, content, programs, products, and services offered by **Ascend Prime Wealth LLC** (“Ascend Prime Wealth,” “Company,” “we,” “our,” or “us”).

By accessing our website, submitting a form, booking a call, purchasing an offer, checking a consent box, or otherwise using our services, you agree to these Terms. If you do not agree, do not use our website or services.

---

## 1. Who We Are

Ascend Prime Wealth LLC  
2909 Wayzata Blvd Ste 1017  
Minneapolis, MN 55405  
support@ascendprimewealth.com

---

## 2. Scope of These Terms

These Terms apply to your use of our public-facing website, funnels, lead forms, communications, and offers unless a separate written agreement, order form, or offer-specific terms apply to a particular purchase or service.

If there is a conflict between these Terms and an offer page, checkout page, written order form, or signed client agreement, the more specific written terms for that offer or service will control to the extent of the conflict.

---

## 3. Services

We provide educational, consulting, advisory, implementation, and related business services, which may include information, strategies, systems, templates, software-related workflows, marketing-related guidance, funding-related education, and other business growth resources.

Unless expressly stated in writing, our services are not customized legal, tax, accounting, investment, lending, or financial advisory services.

We may update, improve, modify, or discontinue portions of our website, content, or services at any time.

---

## 4. Eligibility and Lawful Use

You represent that:
- you are at least 18 years old and legally able to enter into a binding agreement
- all information you provide to us is true, accurate, and current
- you will use our website and services only for lawful purposes
- you will not use our website or services in a way that violates applicable law, the rights of others, or any platform terms that apply to your business

You agree not to use our services to engage in fraud, deception, unlawful solicitation, unlawful credit activity, unlawful advertising, or misuse of third-party data.

---

## 5. No Professional Advice

All information we provide is for general educational and informational purposes only.

We are not your attorney, accountant, tax advisor, lender, broker-dealer, investment advisor, credit bureau, or fiduciary unless expressly stated in a separate signed written agreement.

Nothing on our website or in our services is legal, tax, accounting, investment, or financial advice, and nothing should be relied on as a substitute for advice from a qualified licensed professional who understands your specific situation.

You are solely responsible for the decisions you make based on information we provide.

---

## 6. No Guarantee of Results

We do not guarantee any specific outcome, including any specific:
- revenue
- profit
- savings
- funding amount
- approval
- lead volume
- booked calls
- business growth result
- marketing performance result
- credit, lending, or financing outcome

Any examples, testimonials, case studies, projections, illustrations, or client stories are provided for informational purposes only. They are not promises, guarantees, or representations that you will achieve the same or similar results.

Your results depend on many factors outside our control, including your market, offer, effort, experience, compliance, financial position, implementation, timing, and third-party decisions.

---

## 7. Testimonials, Reviews, and Examples

We may display testimonials, reviews, endorsements, screenshots, case studies, or examples of client experiences or outcomes.

These materials reflect individual experiences and are not intended to represent that all users will achieve the same results.

Where required by law, we may disclose whether an endorsement reflects a paid relationship, discount, incentive, or other material connection.

---

## 8. Purchases, Payments, and Billing

By purchasing a product or service, you agree to pay all fees, charges, and applicable taxes associated with your purchase.

You authorize us and our third-party payment processors to charge your selected payment method for the amounts due under your order, including any payment plan installments you choose at checkout.

If a payment fails, is declined, is reversed, or is not received when due, we may:
- suspend access to services or materials
- delay delivery
- require an alternative payment method
- cancel the applicable order
- recover amounts due through lawful collection efforts

We may use third-party payment processors and commerce tools. Their terms, privacy policies, processing rules, and security practices may also apply to your transaction.

---

## 9. Refunds, Cancellations, and Guarantees

Refund rights, if any, are governed by the written terms presented for the specific offer you purchase, including any offer page, checkout page, order form, written refund policy, or signed agreement.

If a specific offer includes a satisfaction period, refund window, conditional guarantee, or cancellation right, the exact written terms of that offer will control.

Unless a specific written refund or cancellation right is expressly provided for the applicable offer, fees are non-refundable once we have started providing access, scheduling, onboarding, implementation, strategy, consulting, coaching, or digital delivery, except as required by law.

If you believe a billing error has occurred, you agree to contact us promptly at support@ascendprimewealth.com before initiating a chargeback or payment dispute so the issue can be reviewed in good faith.

---

## 10. Client Responsibilities

You are responsible for:
- providing complete, accurate, and current information
- timely responding to reasonable requests for information or materials
- implementing recommendations at your own discretion and risk
- maintaining your own records, compliance procedures, and business operations
- obtaining any legal, financial, tax, regulatory, advertising, or platform-specific advice you need
- ensuring that your own advertising, communications, websites, funnels, data collection, and business practices comply with applicable laws and platform rules

We are not responsible for delays, failures, or losses caused by incomplete information, inaccurate information, your failure to act, your team, your vendors, your platforms, or circumstances outside our reasonable control.

---

## 11. Third-Party Platforms and Services

Our services may involve, reference, integrate with, or rely on third-party tools, websites, platforms, software, payment processors, advertising platforms, CRMs, scheduling systems, AI tools, communications providers, or service providers.

We do not own or control those third-party services, and we are not responsible for:
- their availability
- their performance
- their security
- their content
- their decisions
- their outages
- their fees
- their changing policies or rules

Your use of third-party services is subject to their terms and policies.

---

## 12. Intellectual Property

All content, materials, frameworks, systems, templates, documents, copy, videos, trainings, graphics, branding, workflows, processes, downloads, and other materials we provide are owned by Ascend Prime Wealth LLC or licensed to us and are protected by applicable intellectual property laws.

We grant you a limited, revocable, non-exclusive, non-transferable license to use purchased materials solely for your own internal business use, unless we expressly state otherwise in writing.

You may not, without our prior written consent:
- copy
- reproduce
- modify
- republish
- distribute
- resell
- sublicense
- share with non-authorized third parties
- create derivative works from our materials

---

## 13. License to Use Information You Provide

You grant us a limited, non-exclusive license to use information, materials, branding assets, content, and other inputs you provide to the extent reasonably necessary to deliver the requested services.

You represent that you have the right to provide those materials and that our permitted use of them for service delivery will not violate the rights of any third party.

---

## 14. Confidentiality

We will use commercially reasonable efforts to maintain the confidentiality of non-public information you provide to us that a reasonable person would understand to be confidential, except where disclosure is reasonably necessary to:
- provide the services
- work with our employees, contractors, advisors, or service providers on a need-to-know basis
- comply with legal obligations
- protect our rights, safety, property, or lawful interests
- investigate fraud, chargebacks, security issues, or misuse

This section does not apply to information that:
- is publicly available through no wrongful act by us
- was already lawfully known to us without confidentiality restrictions
- is independently developed without use of your confidential information
- is lawfully received from a third party without breach of duty

---

## 15. Communications

By submitting your contact information to us, you agree that we may send you service-related, administrative, transactional, and account-related communications by email, phone, and text message where permitted by law.

Marketing emails are subject to applicable unsubscribe rights.

Marketing text messages are subject to applicable consent requirements, and you may opt out through reasonable methods, including reply instructions provided in the message where applicable.

Message and data rates may apply.

Your consent to receive communications is not a condition of purchase unless expressly stated where permitted by law.

---

## 16. Privacy

Your use of our website and services is also subject to our Privacy Policy.

If you provide personal information to us or through our forms, funnels, websites, or systems, you agree that we may collect, use, store, and disclose that information as described in our Privacy Policy and as otherwise permitted by law.

---

## 17. Acceptable Use

You may not:
- misuse our website, forms, funnels, or systems
- upload malicious code
- scrape or copy website content without permission
- attempt unauthorized access to our systems
- impersonate another person or entity
- use our materials to compete unfairly or infringe our rights
- make false, misleading, unlawful, or unsupported claims using our materials
- use our services in a manner that creates legal, regulatory, advertising, or platform risk for us

We may suspend or terminate access if we reasonably believe you violated these Terms or applicable law.

---

## 18. Disclaimer of Warranties

To the fullest extent permitted by law, our website, content, and services are provided on an “as is” and “as available” basis.

We disclaim all warranties, whether express, implied, statutory, or otherwise, including implied warranties of merchantability, fitness for a particular purpose, title, and non-infringement.

We do not warrant that our website or services will be uninterrupted, error-free, secure, or suitable for your specific goals.

---

## 19. Limitation of Liability

To the fullest extent permitted by law, Ascend Prime Wealth LLC and its owners, officers, employees, contractors, affiliates, licensors, and service providers will not be liable for any indirect, incidental, special, consequential, exemplary, or punitive damages, or for any loss of profits, revenue, business opportunity, goodwill, data, savings, or reputation arising out of or related to your use of our website, content, or services.

To the fullest extent permitted by law, our total aggregate liability arising out of or related to any claim under these Terms will not exceed the total amount you paid to us for the specific product or service giving rise to the claim during the six-month period preceding the event giving rise to the claim.

Nothing in these Terms excludes liability that cannot lawfully be excluded.

---

## 20. Indemnification

You agree to defend, indemnify, and hold harmless Ascend Prime Wealth LLC and its owners, officers, employees, contractors, affiliates, licensors, and service providers from and against any third-party claims, liabilities, damages, judgments, losses, costs, and expenses, including reasonable attorneys’ fees, arising out of or related to:
- your misuse of our website or services

## 21. Suspension and Termination

We may suspend or terminate your access to all or part of our website or services at any time if:
- you breach these Terms
- you fail to pay amounts due
- your conduct creates legal, reputational, operational, or platform risk
- we suspect fraud, abuse, or unlawful activity
- continued service becomes impracticable, unlawful, or commercially unreasonable

Any provisions that by their nature should survive termination will survive, including payment obligations, intellectual property protections, confidentiality, disclaimers, limitations of liability, indemnification, dispute provisions, and any other accrued rights.

---

## 22. Informal Dispute Resolution

Before filing a lawsuit or initiating formal proceedings, you agree to send written notice of the dispute to support@ascendprimewealth.com with reasonable detail about the issue and the relief requested.

The parties will use good-faith efforts to try to resolve the dispute informally for at least 14 days after the notice is sent before formal proceedings are started, unless urgent injunctive relief is reasonably necessary.

## 23. Governing Law and Venue

These Terms are governed by the laws of the State of Minnesota, without regard to conflict of laws principles.

Unless a signed written agreement for a specific service states otherwise, any dispute arising out of or related to these Terms or your use of our website or services shall be brought exclusively in the state or federal courts located in or serving Hennepin County, Minnesota, and you consent to that jurisdiction and venue.

---

## 24. Force Majeure

We are not liable for delay, interruption, or failure to perform caused by events beyond our reasonable control, including acts of God, internet or utility failures, platform outages, labor disruptions, governmental actions, cyber incidents, natural disasters, pandemics, or third-party service interruptions.

## 25. Changes to These Terms

We may update these Terms from time to time.

The version posted on our website will control as of its stated effective date. Your continued use of our website or services after updated Terms are posted constitutes acceptance of the revised Terms.

---

## 26. General Terms

These Terms constitute the entire agreement between you and Ascend Prime Wealth LLC regarding your use of our public-facing website and services unless superseded by a more specific written agreement.

If any provision of these Terms is held invalid or unenforceable, the remaining provisions will remain in effect.

> 16.2 TERMINATION BY THE CLIENT
> The Client may terminate this Agreement in whole or in part by written notice to ScaleClients.io if
> ScaleClients.io:
> (a) has committed a material breach of this Agreement and has failed to remedy the breach
> within 30 days after receiving written notice from the Client; or
> (b) consents to such termination, subject to the Client’s fulfilment of any pre-conditions to
> such consent (for example, payment of a pro-rata portion of the agreed fees).
> 16.3 EFFECT OF TERMINATION
> Upon termination of this Agreement, the Client must promptly pay (at the ScaleClients.io’s request):
> (a) the Cancellation Fee specified in the Client Form;
> (b) any payments required by ScaleClients.io to third party suppliers or ScaleClients.io to
> discontinue their work;
> (c) ScaleClients.io’s standard fees in relation to work already performed; and/or
> (d) an equitable amount by way of profit margin on the preceding items.
> 16.4 SURVIVAL
> Any clause that by its nature would reasonably be expected to be performed after the termination
> or expiry of this Agreement will survive and be enforceable after such termination or expiry.
> 17 DISPUTE RESOLUTION
> (a) A party claiming that a dispute has arisen under or in connection with this agreement
> must not commence court proceedings arising from or relating to the dispute, other than a
> claim for urgent interlocutory relief, unless that party has complied with the requirements
> of this clause.
> (b) A party that requires resolution of a dispute which arises under or in connection with this
> agreement must give the other party or parties to the dispute written notice containing
> reasonable details of the dispute and requiring its resolution under this clause.
> (c) Once the dispute notice has been given, each party to the dispute must then use its best
> efforts to resolve the dispute in good faith. If the dispute is not resolved within a period of
> 14 days (or such other period as agreed by the parties in writing) after the date of the
> notice, any party to the dispute may take legal proceedings to resolve the dispute.
> 918 NOTICES
> A notice or other communication to a party under this agreement must be:
> (a) in writing and in English; and
> (b) delivered via email to the other party, to the email address specified in this agreement, or
> if no email address is specified in this agreement, then the email address most regularly
> used by the parties to correspond for the purposes of the subject matter of this agreement
> as at the date of this agreement (Email Address). The parties may update their Email
> Address by notice to the other party.
> (c) Unless the party sending the notice knows or reasonably ought to suspect that an email
> was not delivered to the other party’s Email Address, notice will be taken to be given:
> (i) 24 hours after the email was sent, unless that falls on a Saturday, Sunday or a
> public holiday in the state or territory whose laws govern this Agreement, in
> which case the notice will be taken to be given on the next occurring Business
> Day in that state or territory; or
> (ii) when replied to by the other party,
> whichever is earlier.
> 19 GENERAL
> 19.1 GOVERNING LAW AND JURISDICTION
> This agreement is governed by the law applying in Western Australia, Australia. Each party
> irrevocably submits to the exclusive jurisdiction of the courts of Western Australia, Australia and
> courts of appeal from them in respect of any proceedings arising out of or in connection with this
> agreement. Each party irrevocably waives any objection to the venue of any legal process on the
> basis that the process has been brought in an inconvenient forum.

> (c) without limiting any of ScaleClients.io’s other rights under these terms, the Client must

> which payment is received by ScaleClients.io; and
> (d) the Client must reimburse ScaleClients.io for any costs it incurs, including any legal costs,
> in recovering the amount due or enforcing any of its rights under this Agreement.

> Unless otherwise indicated, amounts stated in a Client Form do not include GST. In relation to any
> GST payable for a taxable supply by ScaleClients.io, the Client must pay the GST subject to
> ScaleClients.io providing a tax invoice.

> using a credit, debit or charge card (including Visa, MasterCard or American Express).

> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.

> [!note]- Reasoning recap
> Thought for 26s


---

## 🤖 ChatGPT

Yes — you’re right. I overbuilt it.

## Correct Call

For a **public-facing** page, that draft is **too dense**. It reads more like a backend service agreement than a website terms page.

That matters because public terms should do 3 things well:
1. look normal and credible to prospects  
2. cover the core legal/compliance points  
3. point to the more specific checkout / signed agreement for the hard edges

## What the uploaded docs show

The **shorter ScaleClients-style agreement** is much closer to the right feel for public-facing simplicity: scope, fees, cancellations, no guarantee, guarantee window, confidentiality, termination, governing law. It is compact and readable. fileciteturn6file12

By contrast, the **Prime Mover terms** are much denser and more backend-style, with no-refund language, auto-renew, arbitration, class waiver, detailed SMS terms, indemnity, force majeure, and a full liability section. That is exactly the style that can feel heavy on a public site. fileciteturn6file2 fileciteturn6file10 fileciteturn6file11

The **ScaleClients master contract** also confirms the split: detailed payment processor language, late payment, third-party terms, IP licensing, confidentiality, notices, dispute process, and general boilerplate belong more naturally in the signed agreement layer than the public website layer. fileciteturn6file5 fileciteturn6file8 fileciteturn6file4

## Better structure

### Public-facing Terms
Keep this **lean**. About **8–10 short sections**.

### Checkout / order form terms
Add the more specific offer rules:
- payment plan defaults
- refund window conditions
- chargeback cooperation language
- program-specific deliverables
- timeline / access terms

### Signed client agreement
Put the full protective mechanics there:
- indemnity
- force majeure
- detailed IP license
- detailed confidentiality carveouts
- notices
- survival
- venue / arbitration specifics if used
- collection costs / legal fees if appropriate

## What should stay in the public page

Keep:
- acceptance of terms
- what the services are generally
- no legal / tax / financial advice
- no guarantee of results
- payments and billing basics
- refunds / cancellations by reference to offer-specific terms
- communications / email / SMS basics
- privacy policy reference
- IP basics
- limitation of liability
- dispute notice + governing law
- contact

## What should come out of the public page

Move out of the public page:
- full indemnification
- force majeure
- assignment
- survival
- detailed third-party platform risk allocation
- long acceptable use list
- detailed termination triggers
- long general provisions
- dense “entire agreement / severability / waiver / headings” boilerplate
- aggressive arbitration/class-waiver language unless you specifically want that public

## Best public-facing target

A strong public T&C page should feel like:
- **1,000–1,800 words**
- easy to skim
- plain English
- protective, but not adversarial

The draft I gave was closer to a **contract shell**, not a public-facing page.

## My recommendation

We should replace it with a **lean public version** using this exact structure:

1. Acceptance of Terms  
2. Services and Educational Nature  
3. No Guarantee of Results  
4. Purchases, Payments, and Billing  
5. Refunds, Cancellations, and Offer-Specific Terms  
6. Communications and Privacy  
7. Intellectual Property  
8. Limitation of Liability  
9. Disputes and Governing Law  
10. Contact  

That will be much more aligned with your goal:
- compliant
- professional
- not scary
- still protective

## Bottom line

You were correct to flag it.  
For public-facing use, **we should simplify materially**.

I’ll draft the **lean public-facing T&C v2** next if you want.

> COACHING SERVICES AGREEMENT
> This Coaching Services Agreement (“Agreement”) is entered into as of __________, by and between:
> Coach: Kai Bax, with principal business address at 11 Barrack Square, 6000 (“Coach”),
> And…
> Client: _______________ , residing at _______________________ (“Client”).
> 1. Scope of Services
> Coach agrees to provide coaching services to Client for the purpose of assisting Client in achieving personal,
> professional, and business objectives, as mutually agreed upon (“Services”). Services shall include:
> ● One (1) 60-minute 1-on-1 onboarding session via Zoom upon joining.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Two (2) 60-minute group sessions per week via Zoom with our Sales Coach for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Complete access to training modules on the Skool platform for a term of four (4) months.
> ● Client shall have daily access to the Coach via the group community on the Skool platform for a period of
> four (4) months. The Coach will use commercially reasonable efforts to respond to all questions within
> twenty-four (24) hours.
> 2. Fees and Payment
> ● Fee: Client shall pay Coach a total amount of USD 6,800 for the Services.
> ● Payment Due Date: Payment shall be made in full before commencement of Services, unless otherwise
> agreed in writing.
> ● No Refunds: All fees are non-refundable for any reason, including but not limited to early termination by
> Client, non-attendance, or dissatisfaction with results (subject to Clause 5).
> 3. Cancellations and Rescheduling
> Client must provide at least 12 hours’ prior notice to reschedule a session. Sessions missed without such notice shall
> be deemed forfeited and will not be rescheduled.
> 4. No Guarantee of Results
> Coach does not warrant or guarantee any specific outcomes from the Services. Client acknowledges that results are
> dependent upon Client’s personal effort, actions, and implementation.
> 5. 7-Day Love It or Leave It Guarantee
> Client may cancel this Agreement for any reason within seven (7) calendar days from the Effective Date (“Guarantee
> Period”) by providing written notice to Coach. If such notice is received within the Guarantee Period, Coach shall
> issue a full refund of all amounts paid under this Agreement within seven (7) days of receiving notice. After the
> expiration of the Guarantee Period, all fees paid shall be non-refundable for any reason.
> 02 / 03 / 2026
> Alan Nguyen, Prismatic 10700 France Ave S Ste 110, Bloomingt
> Doc ID: 36474df7a29e92bf63869e8b60f75d604bed108d6. Confidentiality
> Coach shall maintain the confidentiality of all information disclosed by Client during the course of Services, except as
> required by law or with Client’s prior written consent.
> 7. Termination
> Client may terminate this Agreement at any time by providing written notice to the Coach. Upon termination, no
> refunds shall be issued for amounts paid (subject to Clause 5).
> 8. Governing Law
> This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of the State of Perth, Western
> Australia, without regard to its conflict of law provisions.
> IN WITNESS WHEREOF, the parties hereto have executed this Agreement as of the date first written above.
